- Wired one new row into `mobile/app/settings.tsx` → About section only (not Start/match/offer path).
- Added `en`/`ru` i18n strings.
- Committed on `admin/20260808T075248Z-045-open-on-pc-phase1-stub` (no push), following the same convention as the prior `003-offer-thrash` admin commit.

===== attempt 1 / 2 =====
Task complete. Summary:

**What changed:** Filled English-fallback gaps across all 14 `mobile/src/i18n/overlay/*.json` files for the target categories — notify hints, friends Online/Chat/Call, and live `partnerNotReady`/`needStars`. Added/translated ~40 keys natively for de, es, fr, pl, pt, ar, zh, cs, bg, sr, tr (uk needed only 5, ru/en needed none beyond the bug fix below).

**Bugs found and fixed (within the same key set, so in scope):**

- `mobile.live.needStars` had the ★ glyph mojibake-corrupted to `â` in ar/cs/bg/sr — fixed for that key (left 5 other out-of-scope corrupted keys in those files noted for a follow-up task).
